> .theme-black, .theme-dark, .theme-oled {> 
>   --app-bg: #212121;> 
>   --app-card: #2f2f2f;> 
>   --app-text: #f7f7f8;> 
>   --app-text-muted: #b4b4b4;> 
>   --app-border: #424242;> 
>   --app-accent: #66a6ff;> 
>   --app-accent-soft: #2a3a50;> 
> }> 
> > 
> /* Deep Midnight Slate */> 
> .theme-midnight {> 
>   --app-bg: #181a1f;> 
>   --app-card: #24272d;> 
>   --app-text: #f7f7f8;> 
>   --app-text-muted: #a7adb7;> 
>   --app-border: #383d46;> 
>   --app-accent: #67b7ff;> 
>   --app-accent-soft: #203548;> 
> }> 
> > 
> > 
> /* Cross-device shell: iOS/iPadOS safe areas, dynamic viewport, and desktop stability. */> 
> html {> 
>   min-width: 0;> 
>   min-height: 100%;> 
>   background: var(--color-app-bg);> 
>   -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%;> 
>   text-size-adjust: 100%;> 
>   overscroll-behavior-x: none;> 
> }> 
> > 
> body {> 
>   min-width: 0;> 
>   min-height: 100dvh;> 
>   margin: 0;> 
>   overflow-x: hidden;> 
>   overscroll-behavior-x: none;> 
>   -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;> 
> }> 
> > 
> #root {> 
>   min-height: 100dvh;> 
>   min-width: 0;> 
> }> 
> > 
> /* Avoid the classic iOS 100vh jump when the browser chrome opens/closes. */> 
> .min-h-screen { min-height: 100dvh; }> 
> .h-screen { height: 100dvh; }> 
> > 
> /* PWA/browser safe-area helpers. */> 
> .safe-area-top { padding-top: var(--safe-top); }> 
> .safe-area-bottom { padding-bottom: var(--safe-bottom); }> 
> .safe-area-left { padding-left: var(--safe-left); }> 
> .safe-area-right { padding-right: var(--safe-right); }> 
> .safe-area-x { padding-left: var(--safe-left); padding-right: var(--safe-right); }> 
> > 
> /* Touch devices should not inherit desktop hover behavior as their primary interaction. */> 
> @media (hover: none) and (pointer: coarse) {> 
>   .hover\:scale-\[1\.02\]:hover,> 
>   .hover\:scale-\[1\.015\]:hover,> 
>   .hover\:scale-\[1\.01\]:hover {> 
>     transform: none !important;> 
>   }> 
> }> 
> > 
> /* iPhone/iPad home-indicator clearance for fixed bottom controls. */> 
> .fixed-bottom-safe {> 
>   bottom: var(--safe-bottom);> 
>   padding-bottom: max(0.75rem, var(--safe-bottom));> 
> }> 
> > 
> @media (max-width: 767px) {> 
>   /* Keep content readable without creating nested horizontal scroll regions. */> 
>   .studysnap-content {> 
>     width: 100%;> 
>     max-width: 100%;> 
>     overflow-x: clip;> 
>     box-sizing: border-box;> 
>   }> 
> > 
>   /* Never allow a flex/grid child to establish a wider-than-viewport layout. */> 
>   .studysnap-content :where(.flex, .grid) > * {> 
>     min-width: 0;> 
>   }> 
> > 
>   /* Fixed navigation must sit above the iOS home indicator without covering content. */> 
>   .studysnap-content img,> 
>   .studysnap-content video,> 
>   .studysnap-content iframe {> 
>     max-width: 100%;> 
>   }> 
> }> 
> > 
> /* Prevent mobile Safari from turning long unbroken labels/URLs into layout overflow. */> 
> :where(h1,h2,h3,h4,p,span,button,a) {> 
>   overflow-wrap: anywhere;> 
> }> 
> > 
> /* Native-style viewport architecture: the content scrolls in its own pane while the> 
>    navigation occupies real layout space. This prevents mobile overlays rather than> 
>    merely moving them upward. */> 
> .studysnap-app-shell {> 
>   width: 100%;> 
>   max-width: 100vw;> 
>   min-width: 0;> 
>   overscroll-behavior: none;> 
>   box-sizing: border-box;> 
>   padding-top: var(--safe-top);> 
>   padding-left: var(--safe-left);> 
>   padding-right: var(--safe-right);> 
> }> 
> > 
> .studysnap-main {> 
>   width: 100%;> 
>   min-width: 0;> 
>   scrollbar-gutter: stable;> 
>   -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;> 
> }> 
> > 
> .studysnap-main > * {> 
>   min-width: 0;> 
>   max-width: 100%;> 
> }> 
> > 
> /* Professional AI-product workspace chrome: compact, neutral, and persistent. */> 
> .app-topbar {> 
>   position: sticky;> 
>   top: 0;> 
>   z-index: 35;> 
>   min-height: 64px;> 
>   display: flex;> 
>   align-items: center;> 
>   justify-content: space-between;> 
>   gap: 16px;> 
>   padding: 10px 24px;> 
>   background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-bg) 88%, transparent);> 
>   border-bottom: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-border) 75%, transparent);> 
>   backdrop-filter: blur(18px);> 
>   -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(18px);> 
> }> 
> .app-topbar-left, .app-topbar-actions, .app-breadcrumb { display:flex; align-items:center; min-width:0; }> 
> .app-topbar-left { gap: 12px; }> 
> .app-breadcrumb { gap: 8px; font-size: 13px; color: var(--app-text); }> 
> .app-breadcrumb-muted { color: var(--app-text-muted); font-weight: 600; }> 
> .app-breadcrumb strong { font-weight: 750; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }> 
> .app-topbar-actions { gap: 8px; }> 
> .app-topbar-icon, .app-avatar { width: 38px; height: 38px; border-radius: 12px; display:grid; place-items:center; border:1px solid var(--app-border); background:var(--app-card); color:var(--app-text); }> 
> .app-avatar { background:var(--app-accent); color:#fff; border-color:transparent; font-size:13px; font-weight:800; }> 
> .app-command-button { min-height: 38px; min-width: 220px; padding: 0 10px 0 12px; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; border:1px solid var(--app-border); border-radius: 12px; background:var(--app-card); color:var(--app-text-muted); font-size:12px; font-weight:650; text-align:left; box-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.03); }> 
> .app-command-button:hover { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-accent) 40%, var(--app-border)); color:var(--app-text); }> 
> .app-command-button span { flex:1; }> 
> .app-command-button kbd { border:1px solid var(--app-border); border-radius:6px; padding:2px 5px; font-size:9px; font-family:var(--font-mono); color:var(--app-text-muted); }> 
> .app-status-chip { min-height:32px; padding:0 9px; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:6px; border:1px solid var(--app-border); border-radius:999px; color:var(--app-text-muted); background:var(--app-card); font-size:10px; font-weight:750; }> 
> .app-status-dot { width:7px; height:7px; border-radius:50%; background:#a1a1aa; }> 
> .app-status-dot.is-online { background:#22c55e; box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(34,197,94,.12); }> 
> .mobile-menu-trigger { display:none; }> 
> > 
> @media (max-width: 767px) {> 
>   .app-topbar { min-height: 58px; padding: 8px 12px; gap:8px; }> 
>   .mobile-menu-trigger { display:grid; }> 
>   .app-command-button { min-width: 38px; width:38px; padding:0; justify-content:center; }> 
>   .app-command-button span, .app-command-button kbd, .app-status-chip span:last-child { display:none; }> 
>   .app-status-chip { width:34px; justify-content:center; padding:0; }> 
>   .app-breadcrumb-muted, .app-breadcrumb > span:nth-child(2) { display:none; }> 
>   .app-breadcrumb { font-size:14px; }> 
> }> 
> > 
> @media (prefers-reduced-transparency: reduce) {> 
>   .app-topbar { backdrop-filter:none; -webkit-backdrop-filter:none; background:var(--app-bg); }> 
> }> 
> > 
> @media (max-width: 767px) {> 
>   .studysnap-main {> 
>     padding-bottom: 0;> 
>   }> 
> }> 
> > 
> @media (min-width: 768px) {> 
>   .studysnap-main {> 
>     padding-bottom: 0;> 
>   }> 
> }> 
> > 
> > 
> /* Custom Utilities */> 
> .no-scrollbar::-webkit-scrollbar {> 
>   display: none;> 
> }> 
> .no-scrollbar {> 
>   -ms-overflow-style: none;> 
>   scrollbar-width: none;> 
> }> 
> > 
> .glass {> 
>   @apply bg-white/40 backdrop-blur-3xl border border-white/20;> 
> }> 
> > 
> .theme-dark .glass, .theme-midnight .glass, .theme-cyber .glass {> 
>   @apply bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-3xl border border-white/5;> 
> }> 
> > 
> .neural-bg {> 
>   background: > 
>     radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, var(--color-app-accent-soft) 0%, transparent 70%),> 
>     radial-gradient(circle at 0% 0%, var(--color-app-accent-soft) 0%, transparent 40%),> 
>     radial-gradient(circle at 100% 100%, var(--color-app-accent-soft) 0%, transparent 40%);> 
>   background-attachment: fixed;> 
> }> 
> > 
> .cyber-grid {> 
>   background-image: > 
>     linear-gradient(var(--color-app-border) 1px, transparent 1px),> 
>     linear-gradient(90deg, var(--color-app-border) 1px, transparent 1px);> 
>   background-size: 40px 40px;> 
> }> 
> > 
> .card-shadow {> 
>   box-shadow: 0 8px 32px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);> 
> }> 
> > 
> .theme-cyber .card-shadow {> 
>   box-shadow: 0 0 20px -5px var(--color-app-text);> 
> }> 
> > 
> .bento-grid {> 
>   display: grid;> 
>   grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(160px, 1fr));> 
>   gap: 1rem;> 
> }> 
> > 
> .bento-item {> 
>   @apply rounded-[2.5rem] p-6 transition-all duration-500 hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-[0.98] border border-app-border/50;> 
> }> 
> > 
> > 
> /* StudySnap visual system — inspired by the supplied clean sky/purple reference. */> 
> .reference-shell {> 
>   background:> 
>     radial-gradient(circle at 85% 8%, rgba(255,255,255,.95) 0 8%, transparent 28%),> 
>     radial-gradient(circle at 10% 0%, rgba(255,255,255,.7) 0 10%, transparent 30%),> 
>     linear-gradient(180deg, #dcecff 0%, #eef5ff 42%, #f8fbff 100%);> 
> }> 
> .reference-card {> 
>   background: rgba(255,255,255,.94);> 
>   border: 1px solid rgba(224,230,244,.95);> 
>   box-shadow: 0 24px 70px -36px rgba(39,51,104,.28);> 
>   border-radius: 28px;> 
> }> 
> .reference-primary {> 
>   background: linear-gradient(135deg, #4338f2 0%, #3021d6 100%);> 
>   color: #fff;> 
>   box-shadow: 0 18px 40px -18px rgba(59,45,228,.55);> 
> }> 
> .reference-pill {> 
>   border: 1px solid #e7eaff;> 
>   background: rgba(255,255,255,.9);> 
>   border-radius: 999px;> 
> }> 
> .reference-icon {> 
>   width: 48px; height: 48px; border-radius: 16px;> 
>   display: grid; place-items: center;> 
>   background: #eef0ff; color: #3b2de4;> 
> }> 
> :where(button, input[type="button"], input[type="submit"], input[type="reset"]) {> 
>   cursor: pointer;> 
>   touch-action: manipulation;> 
>   -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;> 
> }> 
> :where(button, input, select, textarea):focus-visible {> 
>   outline: 3px solid rgba(59,45,228,.28);> 
>   outline-offset: 2px;> 
> }> 
> @media (max-width: 640px) {> 
>   :where(button, input[type="button"], input[type="submit"], input[type="reset"]) {> 
>     min-height: 48px;> 
>   }> 
> }> 
> body.text-size-small { font-size: 14px; }> 
> body.text-size-normal { font-size: 16px; }> 
> body.text-size-large { font-size: 18px; }> 
> body.text-size-large button, body.text-size-large input, body.text-size-large textarea { font-size: 1em; }> 
> > 
> /* Voice Tutor professional visual layer — clean sky/white/purple product UI. */> 
> .voice-tutor {> 
>   --voice-ink: #0b1230;> 
>   --voice-muted: #64748b;> 
>   --voice-purple: #4338f2;> 
>   --voice-surface: rgba(255,255,255,.88);> 
>   background:> 
>     radial-gradient(circle at 82% 4%, rgba(255,255,255,.98) 0 7%, transparent 27%),> 
>     radial-gradient(circle at 4% 2%, rgba(255,255,255,.72) 0 9%, transparent 28%),> 
>     linear-gradient(180deg, #dcecff 0%, #eef5ff 38%, #f8fbff 100%);> 
>   color: var(--voice-ink);> 
> }> 
> .voice-tutor .reference-card {> 
>   background: rgba(255,255,255,.94);> 
>   border: 1px solid #e2e8f5;> 
>   border-radius: 24px;> 
>   box-shadow: 0 20px 60px -35px rgba(35,49,100,.28);> 
> }> 
> .voice-tutor button { transition: transform .18s ease, box-shadow .18s ease, background-color .18s ease, border-color .18s ease; }> 
> .voice-tutor button:not(:disabled):hover { transform: translateY(-1px); }> 
> .voice-tutor button:not(:disabled):active { transform: translateY(0) scale(.98); }> 
> .voice-tutor button:disabled { cursor: not-allowed; }> 
> .voice-tutor .voice-tutor-hero {> 
>   background: linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(255,255,255,.96), rgba(246,248,255,.82));> 
>   border: 1px solid rgba(224,230,244,.95);> 
>   border-radius: 32px;> 
>   box-shadow: 0 30px 80px -44px rgba(42,54,108,.38);> 
> }> 
> .voice-tutor .voice-primary {> 
>   background: linear-gradient(135deg, #4b3cf5 0%, #3021d6 100%);> 
>   color: #fff;> 
>   box-shadow: 0 20px 42px -20px rgba(59,45,228,.58);> 
> }> 
> .voice-tutor .voice-status {> 
>   background: rgba(255,255,255,.86);> 
>   border: 1px solid #e1e7f5;> 
>   color: #334155;> 
> }> 
> @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {> 
>   .voice-tutor *, .voice-tutor *::before, .voice-tutor *::after { animation-duration: .01ms !important; transition-duration: .01ms !important; }> 
> }> 
> > 
> /* StudySnap navigation principle: normal pages discover content vertically. */> 
> html, body, #root { max-width: 100%; overflow-x: hidden; }> 
> button, a, input, textarea, select { -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent; }> 
> button:focus-visible, a:focus-visible, input:focus-visible, textarea:focus-visible, select:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid rgba(99,102,241,.35); outline-offset: 2px; }> 
> > 
> /* Cross-device app shell: one document scroll, responsive content width,> 
>    and no oversized desktop overlays on phones. */> 
> #root { min-height: 100dvh; }> 
> @media (min-width: 768px) {> 
>   #root > div {> 
>     width: 100%;> 
>   }> 
>   .studysnap-content {> 
>     width: min(100%, 1440px);> 
>     margin-inline: auto;> 
>   }> 
> }> 
> @media (max-width: 767px) {> 
>   body {> 
>     overscroll-behavior-y: contain;> 
>   }> 
>   .glass {> 
>     backdrop-filter: blur(12px);> 
>     -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(12px);> 
>   }> 
>   .reference-card {> 
>     border-radius: 22px;> 
>   }> 
> }> 
> @media (max-width: 420px) {> 
>   .reference-card {> 
>     border-radius: 18px;> 
>   }> 
> }> 
> > 
> /* StudySnap Pro v2 product shell: calm, professional, responsive and content-first. */> 
> :root {> 
>   --app-radius: 18px;> 
>   --app-radius-sm: 14px;> 
>   --app-shadow-soft: 0 12px 36px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.06);> 
>   --app-shadow-card: 0 8px 26px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.07);> 
>   --app-focus: rgba(79, 70, 229, 0.28);> 
> }> 
> .theme-white {> 
>   --app-bg: #f6f7fb;> 
>   --app-card: #ffffff;> 
>   --app-text: #111827;> 
>   --app-text-muted: #667085;> 
>   --app-border: #e6e8ef;> 
>   --app-accent: #4f46e5;> 
>   --app-accent-soft: #eef0ff;> 
> }> 
> .theme-midnight {> 
>   --app-bg: #0b1020;> 
>   --app-card: #11182a;> 
>   --app-text: #f8fafc;> 
>   --app-text-muted: #9aa7bd;> 
>   --app-border: #263149;> 
>   --app-accent: #7c83ff;> 
>   --app-accent-soft: #1c2340;> 
> }> 
> .theme-black {> 
>   --app-bg: #060606;> 
>   --app-card: #101010;> 
>   --app-text: #f5f5f5;> 
>   --app-text-muted: #a3a3a3;> 
>   --app-border: #252525;> 
>   --app-accent: #8b8fff;> 
>   --app-accent-soft: #1b1b1f;> 
> }> 
> > 
> .studysnap-home-v2 { min-height: auto; }> 
> .home-icon-button {> 
>   width: 44px; height: 44px; border-radius: 14px;> 
>   display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;> 
>   color: var(--app-text); background: var(--app-card); border: 1px solid var(--app-border);> 
>   box-shadow: var(--app-shadow-soft); transition: .18s ease;> 
> }> 
> .home-icon-button:hover { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-accent) 35%, var(--app-border)); transform: translateY(-1px); }> 
> .home-hero {> 
>   position: relative; overflow: hidden; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 24px;> 
>   padding: 24px; border-radius: 22px; border: 1px solid var(--app-border);> 
>   background: linear-gradient(135deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-accent) 10%, var(--app-card)) 0%, var(--app-card) 60%);> 
>   box-shadow: var(--app-shadow-card);> 
> }> 
> .home-hero-copy { position: relative; z-index: 1; max-width: 760px; }> 
> .home-kicker { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:7px; font-size:11px; font-weight:800; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.12em; color:var(--app-accent); }> 
> .home-hero h2 { margin: 8px 0 0; font-size: clamp(1.7rem, 3.3vw, 2.5rem); line-height:1.08; font-weight:800; color:var(--app-text); }> 
> .home-hero p { margin: 10px 0 0; max-width: 650px; font-size: 15px; line-height:1.55; color:var(--app-text-muted); }> 
> .home-primary-button, .home-secondary-button {> 
>   min-height: 44px; border-radius: 13px; display:inline-flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; gap:8px;> 
>   font-weight:800; border: 0; touch-action: manipulation; transition:.18s ease;> 
> }> 
> .home-primary-button { margin-top: 18px; padding: 0 16px; background: var(--app-accent); color:#fff; box-shadow: 0 10px 24px color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-accent) 28%, transparent); }> 
> .home-primary-button:hover { transform: translateY(-1px); }> 
> .home-secondary-button { padding: 0 14px; color:var(--app-accent); background:var(--app-accent-soft); white-space:nowrap; }> 
> .home-hero-orb { width: 104px; height:104px; border-radius: 50%; flex: 0 0 auto; display:grid; place-items:center; color:var(--app-accent); background:color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-accent) 10%, transparent); border:1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-accent) 18%, var(--app-border)); }> 
> .home-section-heading { display:flex; align-items:end; justify-content:space-between; gap:16px; margin-bottom:12px; }> 
> .home-section-heading h2 { margin-top:3px; font-size:1.05rem; font-weight:800; color:var(--app-text); }> 
> .home-text-button { min-height:36px; padding:0 8px; display:inline-flex; align-items:center; gap:5px; color:var(--app-accent); font-size:12px; font-weight:800; }> 
> .home-action-grid { display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0,1fr)); gap:12px; }> 
> .home-action-grid > * { min-width:0; }> 
> .home-next-step { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:14px; padding:15px; border:1px solid var(--app-border); background:var(--app-card); border-radius:18px; box-shadow:var(--app-shadow-soft); }> 
> .home-next-icon { width:42px; height:42px; flex:0 0 auto; display:grid; place-items:center; border-radius:13px; background:var(--app-accent-soft); color:var(--app-accent); }> 
> .home-subject-row { display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr)); gap:10px; }> 
> .home-subject-card, .home-empty-state, .home-subject-more { min-height:76px; border-radius:16px; border:1px solid var(--app-border); background:var(--app-card); display:flex; align-items:center; gap:11px; padding:12px; text-align:left; box-shadow:var(--app-shadow-soft); }> 
> .home-subject-card strong { display:block; color:var(--app-text); font-size:13px; font-weight:800; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap; }> 
> .home-subject-card small { display:block; margin-top:3px; color:var(--app-text-muted); font-size:10px; }> 
> .home-subject-icon { width:38px; height:38px; flex:0 0 auto; border-radius:12px; display:grid; place-items:center; color:#fff; }> 
> .home-subject-more { justify-content:center; text-align:center; color:var(--app-accent); font-weight:800; font-size:12px; }> 
> .home-empty-state { justify-content:center; grid-column:1/-1; color:var(--app-text-muted); font-size:12px; }> 
> .home-stats-grid { display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr)); gap:10px; }> 
> .home-stats-grid .bento-item { border-radius:16px; padding:14px; }> 
> .home-more-tools-button { width:100%; min-height:52px; display:grid; grid-template-columns:auto 1fr auto; align-items:center; gap:12px; padding:12px 14px; border:1px solid var(--app-border); border-radius:16px; background:var(--app-card); color:var(--app-text); text-align:left; }> 
> .home-more-tools-button > span:first-child { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; font-size:13px; font-weight:800; }>

> .responsive-nav { margin: 12px; overflow:hidden; }> 
> .responsive-nav-mobile, .responsive-nav-tablet, .responsive-nav-desktop { display:none; }> 
> @media (max-width: 767px) {> 
>   .responsive-nav { left: max(10px, var(--safe-left)); right: max(10px, var(--safe-right)); bottom: max(10px, var(--safe-bottom)); border-radius:18px; }> 
>   .responsive-nav-mobile { display:block; }> 
>   .responsive-nav-items { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:2px; padding:5px; }> 
>   .responsive-nav-item { min-width:0; flex:1; min-height:52px; border-radius:14px; background:transparent; color:var(--app-text-muted); display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:center; justify-content:center; gap:4px; font-size:10px; font-weight:750; }> 
>   .responsive-nav-item.is-active { background:var(--app-accent-soft); color:var(--app-accent); }> 
>   .responsive-nav-menu { width:50px; min-height:52px; flex:0 0 auto; border-radius:14px; display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:center; justify-content:center; gap:3px; background:transparent; color:var(--app-text-muted); border:1px solid transparent; font-size:9px; font-weight:750; }> 
>   .responsive-nav-menu:active { background:var(--app-bg); }> 
>   .responsive-nav-more-label { display:block; line-height:1; }> 
>   .studysnap-main { padding-bottom: 86px !important; }> 
>   .home-action-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap:10px; }> 
>   .home-action-grid > * { min-height:92px; }> 
>   .home-more-tools { display:none; }> 
>   .home-subject-row { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap:10px; }> 
>   .home-subject-card { min-height:84px; padding:10px; gap:8px; }> 
>   .home-subject-icon { width:34px; height:34px; border-radius:10px; }> 
>   .home-next-step { gap:11px; }> 
>   .home-next-step .home-secondary-button { margin-left:0; width:100%; }> 
>   .home-hero { padding:18px; }> 
>   .home-hero p { font-size:13px; line-height:1.45; }> 
>   .home-hero { padding: 20px; }> 
>   .home-hero-orb { width:74px; height:74px; }> 
>   .home-subject-row { grid-template-columns:1fr; }> 
>   .home-stats-grid { grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr)); }> 
>   .home-stats-grid .bento-item { min-width:0; }> 
>   .home-next-step { align-items:flex-start; flex-wrap:wrap; }> 
>   .home-next-step .home-secondary-button { margin-left:56px; width:calc(100% - 56px); }> 
> }> 
> @media (min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1023px) {> 
>   .responsive-nav { top: calc(12px + var(--safe-top)); bottom: calc(12px + var(--safe-bottom)); left:12px; width:74px; margin:0; border-radius:20px; }>

>   .responsive-rail-brand { width:44px; height:44px; display:grid; place-items:center; border-radius:14px; background:var(--app-accent-soft); color:var(--app-accent); margin-bottom:5px; }> 
>   .responsive-rail-item { width:56px; min-height:56px; border-radius:15px; background:transparent; color:var(--app-text-muted); display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:center; justify-content:center; gap:4px; font-size:8px; font-weight:700; }> 
>   .responsive-rail-item.is-active { background:var(--app-accent-soft); color:var(--app-accent); }>

> @media (min-width: 1024px) {> 
>   .responsive-nav { top:0; left:0; bottom:0; width:248px; margin:0; border-radius:0; border-width:0 1px 0 0; box-shadow:none; }>

>   .responsive-sidebar-brand { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px; padding:4px 8px 20px; }>

>   .responsive-sidebar-group p { margin:0 8px 7px; color:var(--app-text-muted); font-size:10px; font-weight:800; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.12em; }>

>   .responsive-sidebar-item.is-active { background:var(--app-accent-soft); color:var(--app-accent); }> 
>   .responsive-sidebar-more { min-height:42px; margin-top:auto; border-radius:12px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; gap:8px; border:1px solid var(--app-border); background:var(--app-card); color:var(--app-text-muted); font-size:12px; font-weight:750; }> 
>   .responsive-sidebar-add { margin-top:8px; min-height:44px; border-radius:12px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; gap:8px; background:var(--app-accent); color:#fff; font-size:13px; font-weight:800; }> 
>   .responsive-sidebar-help { margin-top:10px; padding:10px; display:flex; gap:7px; align-items:flex-start; color:var(--app-text-muted); font-size:10px; line-height:1.4; }> 
>   .studysnap-main { padding-left: 264px !important; }>

> @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {> 
>   *, *::before, *::after { animation-duration: .001ms !important; animation-iteration-count:1 !important; transition-duration:.001ms !important; scroll-behavior:auto !important; }> 
> }>

> > 
> /* Clear, semantic action language: users can distinguish primary, secondary,> 
>    selected, safe and destructive controls at a glance. */> 
> .ss-btn {> 
>   min-height: 44px;> 
>   min-width: 44px;> 
>   display: inline-flex;> 
>   align-items: center;> 
>   justify-content: center;> 
>   gap: 8px;> 
>   padding: 0 14px;> 
>   border-radius: 13px;> 
>   border: 1px solid var(--app-border);> 
>   font-size: 13px;> 
>   font-weight: 800;> 
>   line-height: 1;> 
>   touch-action: manipulation;> 
>   transition: transform .16s ease, border-color .16s ease, background-color .16s ease, box-shadow .16s ease;> 
> }> 
> .ss-btn:not(:disabled):active { transform: translateY(1px) scale(.985); }> 
> .ss-btn:disabled { opacity: .5; cursor: not-allowed; }> 
> .ss-btn-primary { color: #fff; background: var(--app-accent); border-color: var(--app-accent); box-shadow: 0 6px 16px color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-accent) 22%, transparent); }> 
> .ss-btn-secondary { color: var(--app-text); background: var(--app-card); }> 
> .ss-btn-accent { color: var(--app-accent); background: var(--app-accent-soft); border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-accent) 22%, var(--app-border)); }> 
> .ss-btn-selected { color: #fff; background: var(--app-text); border-color: var(--app-text); }> 
> .ss-btn-success { color: #166534; background: #dcfce7; border-color: #bbf7d0; }> 
> .ss-btn-danger { color: #b91c1c; background: #fee2e2; border-color: #fecaca; }> 
> .ss-btn-icon-label { min-width: 44px; }> 
> @media (hover:hover) and (pointer:fine) {> 
>   .ss-btn:not(:disabled):hover { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-accent) 35%, var(--app-border)); box-shadow: 0 4px 14px rgba(15,23,42,.06); }> 
> }> 
> > 
> /* One scroll owner on touch devices. Nested scroll panes are a common source of> 
>    stuck/oversized pages in Android WebView/PWA and mobile Safari. */> 
> @media (hover: none) and (pointer: coarse), (max-width: 767px) {> 
>   html { scroll-padding-top: calc(64px + var(--safe-top)); scroll-padding-bottom: calc(92px + var(--safe-bottom)); }> 
>   body { min-height: 100svh; }> 
>   .studysnap-app-shell { height: auto !important; min-height: 100svh !important; overflow: visible !important; }> 
>   .studysnap-main { overflow: visible !important; height: auto !important; max-height: none !important; }> 
>   .feature-workspace { min-height: auto !important; max-height: none !important; overflow: visible !important; }> 
>   .feature-workspace-scroll { min-height: 0 !important; max-height: none !important; height: auto !important; overflow: visible !important; overscroll-behavior: auto !important; scrollbar-gutter: auto !important; }> 
>   .responsive-nav { max-height: calc(100svh - var(--safe-top) - var(--safe-bottom) - 20px); }> 
>   .studysnap-content { padding-bottom: calc(92px + var(--safe-bottom)) !important; }> 
> }> 
> > 
> /* Very narrow phones: prevent fixed bottom navigation from forcing a second> 
>    horizontal/vertical scroll context. */> 
> @media (max-width: 380px) {> 
>   .responsive-nav { left: 6px !important; right: 6px !important; bottom: max(6px, var(--safe-bottom)) !important; }> 
>   .responsive-nav-items { gap: 0; padding: 4px; }> 
>   .responsive-nav-item { min-height: 50px; font-size: 9px; }> 
>   .responsive-nav-add { width: 42px; height: 42px; }> 
>   .responsive-nav-menu { width: 38px; height: 38px; }> 
> }> 
> > 
> /* Device-adaptive product primitives. These use container queries where a component> 
>    is constrained by a panel, and viewport/pointer queries where the actual device> 
>    interaction model matters. */> 
> .responsive-panel { container: studysnap-panel / inline-size; min-width: 0; }> 
> .responsive-grid {> 
>   display: grid;> 
>   grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);> 
>   gap: clamp(.75rem, 1.5vw, 1.25rem);> 
> }> 
> @container studysnap-panel (min-width: 520px) {> 
>   .responsive-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); }> 
> }> 
> @container studysnap-panel (min-width: 860px) {> 
>   .responsive-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); }> 
> }> 
> .feature-workspace {> 
>   min-height: 0;> 
>   width: 100%;> 
>   max-width: 100%;> 
>   overflow: hidden;> 
>   display: flex;> 
>   flex-direction: column;> 
> }> 
> .feature-workspace-scroll {> 
>   min-height: 0;> 
>   overflow-y: auto;> 
>   overflow-x: clip;> 
>   overscroll-behavior: contain;> 
>   -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;> 
>   scrollbar-gutter: stable;> 
> }> 
> .feature-toolbar {> 
>   flex-wrap: wrap;> 
>   min-width: 0;> 
> }> 
> .feature-toolbar > * { min-width: 0; }> 
> > 
> @media (max-width: 767px) {> 
>   .device-card {> 
>     border-radius: 1.25rem !important;> 
>     padding: 1rem !important;> 
>   }> 
>   .device-wide-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr !important; }> 
>   .device-dense-hide { display: none !important; }> 
>   .desktop-only { display: none !important; }> 
> }> 
> @media (min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1199px) {> 
>   .phone-only { display: none !important; }> 
>   .device-wide-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)) !important; }> 
> }> 
> @media (min-width: 1200px) {> 
>   .phone-only { display: none !important; }> 
>   .desktop-only { display: initial !important; }> 
>   .device-wide-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)) !important; }> 
> }> 
> @media (hover: none) and (pointer: coarse) {> 
>   .device-hover-only { display: none !important; }> 
>   :where(.feature-toolbar button, .feature-toolbar a) { min-height: 48px; }> 
> }> 
> @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {> 
>   .feature-workspace *, .feature-workspace *::before, .feature-workspace *::after {> 
>     scroll-behavior: auto !important;> 
>     animation-duration: .01ms !important;> 
>     transition-duration: .01ms !important;> 
>   }> 
> }>

> > 
> /* Deep clutter pass: reserve a predictable mobile control lane. */> 
> @media (max-width: 767px) {> 
>   .home-action-grid .home-primary-button,> 
>   .home-action-grid .home-secondary-button { min-width: 0; }> 
>   .home-action-grid .home-action-card-title { white-space: normal; }> 
>   .home-more-tools { display: none !important; }> 
>   .responsive-nav { box-shadow: 0 12px 34px rgba(15,23,42,.14); }> 
> }>

> /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------> 
>    StudySnap platform-native Home refresh (2026)> 
>    Shared content, platform-specific composition.> 
>    Android: Material-inspired compact surfaces.> 
>    iOS/iPadOS: quieter grouped cards and bottom tabs/sidebar.> 
>    Windows: Fluent spacing and desktop command hierarchy.> 
>    macOS: sidebar + toolbar-like controls.> 
>    ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */> 
> .home-command {> 
>   display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; gap:24px;> 
>   padding:24px; border:1px solid var(--app-border); border-radius:24px;> 
>   background:var(--app-card); box-shadow:var(--app-shadow-card);> 
> }> 
> .home-command-copy { min-width:0; max-width:760px; }> 
> .home-command h2 { margin:7px 0 0; font-size:clamp(1.65rem,3.4vw,2.45rem); line-height:1.08; font-weight:850; color:var(--app-text); }> 
> .home-command p { margin:9px 0 0; max-width:680px; font-size:14px; line-height:1.55; color:var(--app-text-muted); }> 
> .home-continue {> 
>   margin-top:14px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; gap:16px;> 
>   padding:15px 16px; border:1px solid var(--app-border); border-radius:18px;> 
>   background:var(--app-card); box-shadow:var(--app-shadow-soft);> 
> }> 
> .home-continue-copy { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:12px; min-width:0; }> 
> .home-continue-icon { width:42px; height:42px; flex:0 0 auto; display:grid; place-items:center; border-radius:13px; background:var(--app-accent-soft); color:var(--app-accent); }> 
> .home-continue h2 { margin-top:3px; font-size:15px; font-weight:850; color:var(--app-text); }> 
> .home-continue p:last-child { margin-top:2px; font-size:12px; color:var(--app-text-muted); white-space:nowrap; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; }> 
> .home-progress { padding-top:2px; }> 
> .home-progress-heading { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; margin-bottom:11px; }> 
> .home-progress-heading h2 { margin-top:3px; font-size:1.05rem; font-weight:800; color:var(--app-text); }> 
> .home-tools-link { width:100%; min-height:48px; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; gap:12px; padding:0 13px; border:1px solid var(--app-border); border-radius:14px; background:var(--app-card); color:var(--app-text-muted); font-size:12px; font-weight:800; }> 
> .home-tools-link > span { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; }> 
> > 
> /* Android phones: Material-like hierarchy, compact bottom navigation. */> 
> html[data-platform="android"][data-form-factor="phone"] .home-command {> 
>   border-radius:22px; padding:20px; background:linear-gradient(180deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-accent) 5%, var(--app-card)), var(--app-card));> 
> }> 
> html[data-platform="android"] .home-primary-button { border-radius:14px; }> 
> html[data-platform="android"] .home-action-grid { gap:10px; }> 
> > 
> /* iOS: grouped, quieter surfaces and less visual chrome. */> 
> html[data-platform="ios"] .home-command,> 
> html[data-platform="ipados"] .home-command { box-shadow:none; }> 
> html[data-platform="ios"] .home-command { border-radius:20px; padding:19px; }> 
> html[data-platform="ios"] .home-continue,> 
> html[data-platform="ios"] .home-subject-card,> 
> html[data-platform="ios"] .home-action-grid > * { border-radius:17px; }> 
> html[data-platform="ios"] .home-primary-button { border-radius:14px; box-shadow:none; }> 
> html[data-platform="ios"] .responsive-nav { border-radius:22px; }> 
> > 
> /* iPad: use a left navigation rail rather than a stretched phone bar. */> 
> html[data-platform="ipados"][data-form-factor="tablet"] .responsive-nav { width:78px; }> 
> html[data-platform="ipados"][data-form-factor="tablet"] .studysnap-main { padding-left:92px !important; }> 
> > 
> /* Windows: Fluent-inspired spacing, clear grouping, desktop-first hierarchy. */> 
> html[data-platform="windows"] .responsive-nav { background:color-mix(in srgb, var(--app-card) 94%, transparent); }> 
> html[data-platform="windows"] .responsive-sidebar-item { border-radius:8px; }> 
> html[data-platform="windows"] .home-command { border-radius:12px; }> 
> html[data-platform="windows"] .home-continue,> 
> html[data-platform="windows"] .home-subject-card,> 
> html[data-platform="windows"] .home-action-grid > * { border-radius:10px; }> 
> html[data-platform="windows"] .home-primary-button { border-radius:8px; }> 
> > 
> /* macOS: calm desktop shell with sidebar-style navigation and lighter controls. */> 
> html[data-platform="macos"] .responsive-sidebar-item { border-radius:8px; }> 
> html[data-platform="macos"] .home-command { border-radius:16px; box-shadow:0 8px 28px rgba(0,0,0,.05); }> 
> html[data-platform="macos"] .home-primary-button { border-radius:9px; }> 
> html[data-platform="macos"] .home-icon-button { border-radius:10px; }> 
> > 
> /* Desktop: avoid a narrow mobile-like column; let content breathe. */> 
> @media (min-width:1200px) {> 
>   .studysnap-home-v2 { max-width:1180px; padding-top:28px; }> 
>   .home-action-grid { grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr)); }> 
>   .home-subject-row { grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr)); }> 
> }> 
> > 
> @media (max-width:767px) {> 
>   .studysnap-home-v2 { padding-top:16px; }> 
>   .home-command { display:block; padding:18px; border-radius:20px; }> 
>   .home-command h2 { font-size:1.55rem; }> 
>   .home-command p { font-size:13px; }> 
>   .home-primary-button { width:100%; margin-top:15px; }> 
>   .home-continue { align-items:flex-start; flex-wrap:wrap; padding:14px; }> 
>   .home-continue-copy { width:100%; }> 
>   .home-continue .home-secondary-button { width:100%; margin-left:54px; }> 
>   .home-action-grid { grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr)); }> 
>   .home-action-grid > * { min-height:82px; }> 
>   .home-subject-row { grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr)); }> 
>   .home-subject-card { min-height:74px; }> 
> }> 
> > 
> @media (max-width:380px) {> 
>   .home-action-grid > * { min-height:72px; }> 
>   .home-subject-row { grid-template-columns:1fr; }> 
>   .home-command h2 { font-size:1.4rem; }> 
> }>
